Let's design continuous `pair_adam` in detail:

Update y via Adam on gradient of F. Need compute gradient of weighted sum F_w = ∑ w_h b_h:
```
grad_a = 2 * fft_grad(w, a)  # length n, derivative of F_w wrt a
# pair x gradient:
gx = (grad_a[:m] - grad_a[m:][::-1]) / m? Because x derivative with t=1/m. Actually a_i = x_i/m, a_{n-1-i}=(1-x_i)/m. da_i/dx=1/m, da_{n-1-i}/dx=-1/m. grad_x_i = (1/m)(grad_a_i - grad_a_{n-1-i}).
```
plus constraints. Then y gradient = grad_x * x*(1-x) (since dx/dy). Adam. Step size.

- n even, m=n//2. Extract x from a0? If a0 not exactly anti-pair, we can symmetrize: x_i = m*a_i (for first m) clipped [0,1]? For general, maybe optimize general separately. For pair start from binary. y0=logit(x).
- Maintain a, y, b, score, best.
- Each iteration:
  - compute b=_fft_square(a). score=2n*maxb (normalized sum 1). If score<best update.
  - if time low break.
  - set u = L*b, u_max = max(u)
  - weights = (u/(u_max+eps)) ** (p-1) maybe; plus small floor 1e-6 to touch all.
  - grad_a = 2*fft_grad(weights, a)
  - grad_x = (grad_a[:m] - grad_a[m:][::-1]) / m
  - grad_y = grad_x * x*(1-x)
  - adam step y with lr, beta1, beta2, eps. Clip y to [-20,20] to avoid extremes? But binary extremes may be useful; allow [-30,30]. Clip x [1e-12,1-1e-12].
  - a = from x.
  - optional line search? We can accept if objective weighted improved? Adam should.
  - periodically increase p or use top-peak weights.
```

```
def _apply_flip(A, C, S, n, r):
    p = int(A[r]); q = n-1-p
    idx1 = p + A  # int array length m
    C[idx1] -= 2
    C[2*p] += 1
    idx2 = q + A
    C[idx2] += 2
    C[n-1] -= 2
    C[2*q] += 1
    A[r]=q
    S[p]=0; S[q]=1
```

For gradient B:
```
def _corr_weights(S, W, n):
    # S length n float, W length 2n-1 float
    L = 2*n-1
    N = _pow2(n + L - 1)
    fs = np.fft.rfft(S, N)
    Wrev = W[::-1]
    fw = np.fft.rfft(Wrev, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fs*fw, N).real
    # B[a] = conv[L-1-a], a=0..n-1
    return conv[n-1:L][::-1]
```
Check slice: L=2n-1, conv length N. Need indices n-1..L-1 inclusive length n. Python slice `conv[n-1:L]` where L=2n-1, stop exclusive 2n-1 gives last index 2n-2. Good. Then reverse. For n=8192, length 8192. Good.

delta = 2.0*(B[q] - B[p]) + W[2*p] - 2.0*W[n-1] + W[2*q]
```
Here W indexed 2*p and 2*q max 4m-2=2n-2. Good. W can be from C counts. If W floating, delta float.

Implement x optimizer with Adam:
```
m=a.size//2
x = initial in [1e-6,1-1e-6]
state m,v
for it:
 compute b,u,w,grad_a,gx = (grad_a[:m] - grad_a[m:][::-1]) # no /m? grad_x raw includes /m; normalize cancels.
 g = gx / (np.max(np.abs(gx)) + eps)  # or RMS
 # Adam on g: mstep = b1*mstep+(1-b1)g; v...; x -= lr * mg/sqrt(v)
 x = clip(x, eps,1-eps)
 a = from x
```

Continuous optimizer `pair_moment(x0, time, p=4, lr=0.2, seed)`:
```
eps=1e-8
x=clip
best_x, best_score inf
t0
m? weights schedule: p_values [2,4,8,16]; start p=p0, increase every e.g. 500 iters if score improved? Or fixed.
It loop while time:
  a = from_x(x)
  b = _fft_square(a), u=(2n-1)*b, score=2n*u.max()/L? or internal.
  if score<best update x
  w = (u/(u.max()+eps))**(p-1); w += 1e-8
  grad_a=2*_fft_grad(w,a)
  gx=grad_a[:m]-grad_a[m:][::-1]
  g_scale=np.max(np.abs(gx)); if g_scale<eps: break
  g=gx/g_scale
  Adam x step lr
  x=clip
  # occasional random nudge
  # if no improve for many: restart from best with random perturbation or change p
```

`_fft_grad(weights, a)`:
```
def _fft_grad(weights, a):
    n = int(a.size); L=int(weights.size)
    N = _pow2(L+n-1)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(weights, N)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), N)[:n]
    return 2.0*c
```
This is derivative of ∑ weights*b? For real sequences, gradient wrt a_i = 2∑_j a_j weights[i+j]? The cross-correlation with weights using `fw * conj(fa)`? Let's verify. We need g_i = 2∑_j a_j w_{i+j}. If define convolution a * rev(w)? For linear convolution, (a * w_rev)[i+?]. Our previous code used `fw * np.conj(fa)`? For real, conj not same as product. Let's test formula. We want g_i = 2 ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j w_{i+j}. This is cross-correlation. Standard: use FFT of a and reversed w: conv = a * w_rev, g = conv[n-1?]. But previous code did `fw * np.conj(fa)`. Since fa is DFT of a padded, conj(fa) = DFT of reversed a? For real circular? Does that compute correlation? Let's verify simple: n=2, a=[a0,a1], w=[w0,w1,w2]. g0=2(a0 w0 + a1 w1), g1=2(a0 w1 + a1 w2). Cross-corr c_i =∑ a_j w_{i+j}. Let W_rev[k]=w[L-1-k]. Convolution (a * W_rev)[t] =∑_j a_j W_rev[t-j]=∑ a_j w[L-1-t+j]. Set i = L-1-t+? For t? If t=L-1-i? Then w[i+j]. So g_i=conv[L-1-i]. This is not `fw*conj(fa)`. `fw*conj(fa)` corresponds to correlation of weights and a in circular frequency: inverse of W*conj(A) = ∑ a_j w_{i+j}? For circular? Let's check. For real circular, (W conv circular a) vs W*conj(A) gives cross-correlation: (w ⋆ a)[k]=∑_j w_{k+j} a_j (with indices mod N). That's exactly ∑ a_j w_{k+j}. Yes. Linear if N large enough no wrap; for k=0..n-1, i+j< L ≤ N so no wrap. So `irfft(fw * conj(fa), N)[:n]` gives circular correlation c_i=∑ a_j w_{i+j}. Good. Then g=2c. Great. `fw * fa` would compute convolution with opposite order? For a*a, `_fft_square` uses `fa*fa` because product gives convolution (since both same). For correlation with weights, use `fw * conj(fa)` if fw and fa padded. Good.

But for gradient of F=∑ w_h b_h =∑ w_h∑_i,j a_i a_j δ_{i+j=h}, derivative wrt a_k = ∑_j (w_{k+j} a_j + w_{j+k} a_j?) Since b_h =∑_i a_i a_{h-i}; derivative 2a_k? More correctly ∂/∂a_k ∑_h w_h∑_i a_i a_{h-i} = 2∑_i a_i w_{k+i}. So `c_k=∑ a_i w_{k+i}`, good.

def _pair_moment_opt(x0, time_limit, p=4, lr=0.15, rng=None, focus_floor=1e-6, label="", every=100):
    start=time.monotonic()

    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=inf
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    mstate=np.zeros(m); vstate=np.zeros(m)
    it=0; no_improve=0; max_no=400; resets=0
    current_score=inf
    while time-start < time_limit:
        b=_fft_square(a); u=(2*n-1)*b; smax=float(u.max()); score=float(2*n*smax)/((float(a.sum())**2)?) But a sum may drift due x? Pair sum exactly1 using x, so score=2*n*maxb. Since b from a, sum a=1, use 2*n*maxb.
        current_score=score
        if score<best_score: update best_a,x; no_improve=0
        else no_improve++
        if verbose...
        if no_improve>max_no:
            # perturb from best
            x=best_x + rng.normal(0,0.1,m); clip; a=...
            continue
        w = (u/(smax+1e-12)) ** (p-1) if p>1 else u
        w += 1e-9
        grad_a=2*_fft_grad(w,a)
        gx=grad_a[:m] - grad_a[m:][::-1]
        gscale=np.max(np.abs(gx))
        if not finite: x=best_x+noise; continue
        if gscale<1e-14: break
        g=gx/gscale
        it+=1
        b1t=beta1**it; b2t=beta2**it
        mstate=beta1*mstate+(1-beta1)*g
        vstate=beta2*vstate+(1-beta2)*(g*g)
        mh=mstate/(1-b1t); vh=vstate/(1-b2t)
        x = x - lr * mh / np.sqrt(vh+eps)
        x=np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1-1e-8)
        a=_pair_from_x(x)
    return best_a, best_score, x? 
```

def _sign_greedy(m, mode=0, seed=1):
    n=2*m; L=2*n-1
    C=np.zeros(L,dtype=np.int32)
    A=np.empty(m,dtype=np.int32)

    elif mode==1: order=np.arange(m)
    else random shuffle
    for t,r in enumerate(order):
       left=r; right=n-1-r

       else:
          Acur=A[:t] (int32)
          vL=C[left + Acur] + 2
          mL=max(int(C.max()), int(vL.max()), C[2*left]+1)
          vR=C[right + Acur] + 2
          mR=...
          # tie: sum high; also consider future? choose lower max, then lower sum of v where v>Cmax
       update C, A[t]
    return A,Cmax

Let's design tasks list with allocations (seconds):

def _init_x_fourier(m, rng, amp=0.5, nfreq=6):
    t=np.arange(m,dtype=float)
    x=np.full(m,0.5)
    for f in range(1,nfreq+1):
       amp_f=amp/(f**0.7)
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       x += amp_f*np.sin(2π*f*t/m + phase)
    x=np.clip(x,1e-6,1-1e-6)
    return x
```

`_full_moment_opt`:
```
def _full_moment_opt(a0, time_limit, p=4, lr=0.15, rng=None, label=""):
   a clean normalized
   best_a, best_score inf
   t0; no_imp; resets
   while time:
      b=_fft_square(a); score=...
      update
      W = (u/umax)**(p-1) + 1e-10
      grad_a=2*_fft_grad(W,a)
      gd=grad_a - float(np.dot(a, grad_a))  # zero mean weighted? sum a*gd=0 if dot.
      maxg=np.max(np.abs(gd)); if small: perturb
      gd=gd/maxg
      a = a * np.exp(-lr*gd)
      a=np.maximum(a,1e-300); ss=a.sum(); if ss<=... a=ones/n else a/=ss
      # periodic random multiply: if no improve, reset a=best_a*exp(noise)
```
Dot(a,grad) not mean; zero weighted sum ensures multiplicative update approx preserves sum? Sum of log changes ∑gd not zero, but normalization fixes. Weighted zero helps not drift mass. Previous used `np.dot(a,grad)`. Good.

def _safe_evaluate(seq):
    ev=globals().get("evaluate_sequence")
    if not callable(ev): return inf
    try: return float(ev(seq))
    except: return inf
```

def _init_x_block(m,rng,k=4,base=0.9):
   block=m//k; x=np.empty(m)

   # randomize block lengths/order

```

Now, code details. Let's write functions:

```
def _clean_array(arr):
   a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float).ravel()
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s>0.01: a/=s
   elif a.size>0: a=np.ones_like(a); a/=a.size
   return a
```

`_internal_score(a)`:
```
def _internal_score(a):
    n=a.size; b=_fft_square(a); s=float(a.sum())
    if s<=0.01: return float('inf'), b
    return float(2*n*float(b.max())/(s*s)), b
```

def _random_positive(n, rng, amp=3.0):
   z=rng.normal(0,amp,n); z-=z.max(); a=np.exp(z); a/=a.sum(); return a
```

Detailed code snippets:

```
def _pow2(x):
    x=int(x)
    if x<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()
```

`_fft_square`:
```
def _fft_square(a):
    n=int(a.size); L=2*n-1; N=_pow2(L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,n=N).real[:L]
    np.maximum(b,0.0,out=b)
    return b
```
For integer S, fa*fa may have imaginary small. Good.

`_fft_grad`:
```
def _fft_grad(weights,a):
    n=int(a.size); L=int(weights.size); N=_pow2(L+n-1)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(weights,n=N)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,n=N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conj(fa),n=N).real[:n]
    return 2.0*c
```

`_corr_weights`:
```
def _corr_weights(S,W,n):
    L=2*n-1; N=_pow2(n+L-1)
    fs=np.fft.rfft(S,n=N)
    Wrev = W[::-1]
    fw=np.fft.rfft(Wrev,n=N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fs*fw,n=N).real
    return conv[n-1:L][::-1]
```
Could optimize by using S float64. W float64. If S is bool, rfft converts. Good.

`_init_x_fourier(m,rng,mode=0)`:
Use mode 0..3:
- 0: one sine
- 1: two sines freq1 and freq3
- 2: freq2 and freq5 with random phases
- 3: high frequency? We'll implement switch.
```
def _init_x_fourier(m,rng,mode=0):
    t=np.arange(m,dtype=float); out=np.full(m,0.5)
    if mode==0:
       out=0.5+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*t/m + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    elif mode==1:
       out=0.5+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*t/m + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)) + 0.15*np.sin(6*np.pi*t/m + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    elif mode==2:
       out=0.5+0.4*np.sin(4*np.pi*t/m + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)) + 0.3*np.sin(10*np.pi*t/m + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    else:
       out=0.5+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*3*t/m + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    out=out-rng.uniform(0.0,0.05)*? add noise
    out+=rng.normal(0,0.02,m)
    return np.clip(out,1e-6,1-1e-6)
```

`_init_x_block(m,rng,k=4,val=0.85)`:
```
def _init_x_block(m,rng,k=4,val=0.85):
   k=max(2,min(m,k))
   lengths = rng.integers(m//k, m//k+2, size=k) maybe simpler equal.
   x=np.empty(m)
   i=0
   for j in range(k):
      end=min(m,i+m//k)
      v=val if j%2==0 else 1-val
      x[i:end]=v
      i=end
   x += rng.normal(0,0.05,m)
   return np.clip(x,1e-6,1-1e-6)
```
Use random block start? okay.

z=rng.normal(0,1,m); z-=z.mean(); std=z.std(); if std>0: z=z/std; x=0.5+0.5*np.tanh(z); return clip
```

def _sign_greedy(m, mode=0, rng=None):
    n=2*m; L=2*n-1
    C=np.zeros(L,dtype=np.int32)
    sel=np.empty(m,dtype=np.int64)
    cur_pos=np.empty(m,dtype=np.int64)
    t=0; cur_max=0
    if mode==0: order=np.arange(m-1,-1,-1,dtype=np.int64)
    elif mode==1: order=np.arange(m,dtype=np.int64)
    else: order=np.arange(m,dtype=np.int64); np.random? use rng.permutation
    for r in order:
       left=int(r); right=n-1-left
       if t==0:
          p=left
       else:
          Ac=cur_pos[:t]
          vL=C[left + Ac] + 2
          mL=max(cur_max, int(vL.max()), int(C[2*left]+1))
          vR=C[right + Ac] + 2
          mR=max(cur_max, int(vR.max()), int(C[2*right]+1))
          if mL < mR: p=left
          elif mL > mR: p=right
          else:
              hL=int(np.count_nonzero(vL >= mL)) + int(C[2*left] >= mL)
              hR=int(np.count_nonzero(vR >= mR)) + int(C[2*right] >= mR)
              p=left if hL <= hR else right
       C[p + cur_pos[:t]] += 2
       C[2*p] += 1
       cur_max = max(cur_max, int(C[p+cur_pos[:t]]? after update?), int(C[2*p]))
```

def _sign_to_x(A, left_val=0.9):

`_sign_optimize` code. Need function `_apply_sign_flip(A,C,S,n,r)`.
```
def _apply_sign_flip(A,C,S,n,r):
    p=int(A[r]); q=n-1-p
    idx1 = p + A
    C[idx1] -= 2
    C[2*p] += 1
    idx2 = q + A
    C[idx2] += 2
    C[n-1] -= 2
    C[2*q] += 1
    A[r]=q
    S[p]=0.0; S[q]=1.0
```
Need A as int64; C int32. Since `idx1` uses A before A[r] change. Good.

`_sign_optimize`:
```
def _sign_optimize(A0, time_limit, rng=None, q_values=(2,4,8), batch=8, label=""):
    n=2*A0.size; m=A0.size; L=2*n-1
    A=A0.astype(np.int64).copy()
    S=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64); S[A]=1.0
    C=np.zeros(L,dtype=np.int32)
    # compute C via FFT for reliability
    C=_fft_square(S).astype(np.int32)
    C=np.maximum(C,0).astype(np.int32)
    best_A=A.copy(); best_C=C.copy(); best_S=S.copy()
    Cmax=int(C.max()); best_count=int(np.count_nonzero(C==Cmax))
    q_idx=0; no_imp=0; max_no=250; resets=0
    t0=time.monotonic(); it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        Cmax=int(C.max())
        if Cmax < best_cmax or (Cmax==best_cmax and best_count larger): update best
        if no_imp > max_no: reset...
        q=q_values[min(q_idx,len(q_values)-1)]
        Cmaxf=max(1.0,float(Cmax))
        W=(C.astype(np.float64)/Cmaxf) ** q
        W += 1e-12
        B=_corr_weights(S,W,n)
        p=A; qpos=n-1-p
        delta=2.0*(B[qpos]-B[p]) + W[2*p] - 2.0*W[n-1] + W[2*qpos]
        finite delta; if not, reset
        if np.any(delta < -1e-9):
            idxs=np.argsort(delta)[:batch]
            for idx in idxs:
                if delta[idx] < -1e-12:
                    _apply_sign_flip(A,C,S,n,int(idx))
        else:
            # random few
            for _ in range(rng.integers(1,4)):
                r=int(rng.integers(m)); _apply_sign_flip(A,C,S,n,r)
        # after batch, check C negative? if min<0 due bug? set zeros? But shouldn't.
        if C.min() < 0: C=np.clip(C,0,L?); S? hmm not.
        it+=1
    return best_A, best_C.astype? best_score
```

Now, pair optimizer exact pseudo with return best_x:
```
def _pair_moment_opt(x0, time_limit, p=4, lr=0.15, rng=None, label=""):
    start=time.monotonic()
    m=x0.size; n=2*m
    x=np.asarray(x0,float).copy(); x=np.clip(x,1e-7,1-1e-7)
    best_x=x.copy(); best_a=_pair_from_x(x); best_score=inf
    a=best_a.copy()
    m_acc=np.zeros(m); v_acc=np.zeros(m)
    beta1=0.9; beta2=0.999; eps=1e-8
    it=0; no_imp=0; max_no=500; resets=0
    while time-start < time_limit:
       b=_fft_square(a); score=float(2*n*float(b.max())) # a sum 1
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=score; best_x=x.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); no_imp=0
       else: no_imp +=1
       if verbose and it%200==0: print
       if no_imp > max_no:
          resets+=1
          if resets > 25: break
          if best_score < inf:
              x = best_x + rng.normal(0, 0.15, m)
          else:
              x = _init_x_random(m,rng)
          x=np.clip(x,1e-7,1-1e-7); a=_pair_from_x(x)
          no_imp=0; max_no=int(max_no*1.3)
          continue
       L=2*n-1; u=L*b; smax=float(u.max())
       if smax <= 1e-300: break
       w=(u/(smax+1e-300)) ** max(0,p-1)
       w += 1e-12
       grad_a=2.0*_fft_grad(w,a)
       gx=grad_a[:m] - grad_a[m:][::-1]
       gscale=float(np.max(np.abs(gx)))
       if not np.isfinite(gscale) or gscale < 1e-16:
          x = best_x + rng.normal(0,0.05,m); x=clip; a=...; continue
       g=gx/gscale
       it +=1
       b1t=beta1**it; b2t=beta2**it
       m_acc=beta1*m_acc+(1-beta1)*g
       v_acc=beta2*v_acc+(1-beta2)*(g*g)
       mh=m_acc/(1-b1t); vh=v_acc/(1-b2t)
       x = x - lr * mh / np.sqrt(vh + eps)
       x=np.clip(x,1e-7,1-1e-7)
       a=_pair_from_x(x)
    return best_a, best_score, best_x
```
Potential: If p integer but we pass p=2, exponent1. If p maybe 1.5? not. Use `float(p)`.

As p high, weights peak 1, others tiny. Grad may focus on single max. Good.

def _full_moment_opt(a0, time_limit, p=4, lr=0.15, rng=None, label=""):
   a=_clean_array(a0)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_score=_internal_score(a)[0]
   it=0; no=0; max_no=500; resets=0
   while time:
      b,score,_? = _internal_score(a) # uses FFT
      update
      if no>... reset
      L=2*n-1; u=L*b; smax=u.max(); w=(u/(smax+eps))**max(0,p-1)+1e-12
      grad_a=2*_fft_grad(w,a)
      gd=grad_a - float(np.dot(a,grad_a))
      gscale=max abs; if small reset
      gd=gd/gscale
      with errstate: a=a*np.exp(-lr*gd)
      a=np.maximum(a,1e-300); ss=a.sum(); if ss<=... a=zeros? else a/=ss
      # occasional if too many zeros? no
```

    block=m//k

       v = val if j%2==0 else 1-val

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

Main ideas:
- Work primarily in an anti-palindromic ansatz:
      a_i + a_{n-1-i} = 1/m,  n = 2m.
  This keeps the center coefficient low and exposes m variables x_i in [0,1].
- Minimize flattened moments of the normalized convolution:
      u = (2n-1)*conv(a,a),  mean(u)=1,
  using smooth objectives sum_h u_h^p with focused gradient weights.
- Construct strong initial anti-palindromic binary supports by greedy sign
  construction, then refine with a discrete sign-gradient search.
- Refine the continuous anti-palindromic variables with Adam on normalized
  gradient. Also use a general non-anti log-space exponentiated gradient
  optimizer as a final broad search.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_square(a):
    """Linear convolution a * a via FFT."""
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _pow2(L)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=N).real[:L]
    np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
    return b


def _fft_grad(weights, a):
    """
    Gradient of sum_h weights[h] * conv(a,a)[h] with respect to a.
    Returns 2 * (a correlated with weights).
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(weights.size)
    N = _pow2(L + n - 1)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(weights, n=N)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), n=N).real[:n]
    return 2.0 * c


def _score_norm(a):
    """Score for an arbitrary (possibly unnormalized) array."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    b = _fft_square(a)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(b.max()) / (s * s))


def _clean_array(arr):
    """Clean and normalize an array to a valid candidate array."""
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.01:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    a /= s
    return a


def _pair_from_x(x):
    """Build anti-palindromic normalized sequence from x in [0,1]^m."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(x.size)
    n = 2 * m
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[:m] = x / float(m)
    a[m:] = (1.0 - x[::-1]) / float(m)
    return a


def _random_positive(n, rng, amp=3.0):
    z = rng.normal(0.0, amp, size=n)
    z -= float(z.max())
    a = np.exp(z)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15)
    a /= float(a.sum())
    return a


def _init_x_random(m, rng):
    z = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=m)
    z -= float(z.mean())
    std = float(z.std())
    if std > 1e-12:
        z = z / std
    x = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(1.5 * z)
    return np.clip(x, 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)


def _init_x_fourier(m, rng, mode=0):
    t = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if mode == 0:
        x = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t / m + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
    elif mode == 1:
        x = (
            0.5
            + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t / m + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            + 0.15 * np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * t / m + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        )
    elif mode == 2:
        x = (
            0.5
            + 0.4 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t / m + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            + 0.3 * np.sin(10.0 * np.pi * t / m + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        )
    elif mode == 3:
        x = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(
            6.0 * np.pi * t / m + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        )
    else:
        x = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(
            2.0 * np.pi * t / m + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        )
    x += rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, size=m)
    return np.clip(x, 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)


def _init_x_block(m, rng, k=4, val=0.85):
    k = int(max(2, min(m, k)))
    block = max(1, m // k)
    x = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = 0
    for j in range(k):
        end = m if j == k - 1 else min(m, idx + block)
        v = val if (j % 2 == 0) else (1.0 - val)
        x[idx:end] = v
        idx = end
    x += rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=m)
    return np.clip(x, 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)


def _sign_greedy(m, mode=0, rng=None):
    """
    Greedily choose one position from each antipodal pair.
    Returns selected positions A indexed by pair r=0..m-1, convolution C, max C.
    """
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    n = 2 * m
    L = 2 * n - 1
    C = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.int32)
    selected = np.empty(m, dtype=np.int64)
    current = np.empty(m, dtype=np.int64)
    t = 0
    cur_max = 0

    if mode == 0:
        order = np.arange(m - 1, -1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
    elif mode == 1:
        order = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    elif mode == 2:
        order = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
        rng.shuffle(order)
    else:
        # alternating: center, end, next center, next end, ...
        center = np.arange(m - 1, -1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
        end = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
        order = np.empty(m, dtype=np.int64)
        i = 0
        for a, b in zip(center[::2], end[::2]):
            order[2 * i] = a
            order[2 * i + 1] = b
            i += 1
        if m % 2 == 1:
            order[-1] = center[-1]

    for r in order:
        left = int(r)
        right = n - 1 - left
        Ac = current[:t]

        if t == 0:
            p = left
        else:
            vL = C[left + Ac] + 2
            mL = max(cur_max, int(vL.max()), int(C[2 * left] + 1))

            vR = C[right + Ac] + 2
            mR = max(cur_max, int(vR.max()), int(C[2 * right] + 1))

            if mL < mR:
                p = left
            elif mR < mL:
                p = right
            else:
                hL = int(np.count_nonzero(vL >= mL)) + int(C[2 * left] >= mL)
                hR = int(np.count_nonzero(vR >= mR)) + int(C[2 * right] >= mR)
                p = left if hL <= hR else right

        if t == 0:
            C[2 * p] += 1
        else:
            C[p + Ac] += 2
            C[2 * p] += 1

        current[t] = p
        selected[r] = p
        t += 1
        cur_max = int(C.max())

    return selected, C, cur_max


def _sign_to_x(A, left_val=0.85):
    """Convert selected anti-symmetric positions A to continuous x variables."""
    A = np.asarray(A, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    m = int(A.size)
    n = 2 * m
    left = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
    sel_left = (A == left)
    x = np.where(sel_left, left_val, 1.0 - left_val)
    return np.clip(x, 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)


def _apply_sign_flip(A, C, S, n, r):
    """Flip selected position in pair r from p to n-1-p."""
    p = int(A[r])
    q = n - 1 - p

    idx1 = p + A
    C[idx1] -= 2
    C[2 * p] += 1

    idx2 = q + A
    C[idx2] += 2
    C[n - 1] -= 2
    C[2 * q] += 1

    A[r] = q
    S[p] = 0.0
    S[q] = 1.0


def _corr_weights(S, W, n):
    """
    Given selected indicator S length n and weights W length 2n-1,
    compute B[p] = sum_y S[y] W[p+y], p=0..n-1.
    """
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = _pow2(n + L - 1)
    fs = np.fft.rfft(S, n=N)
    Wrev = W[::-1]
    fw = np.fft.rfft(Wrev, n=N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fs * fw, n=N).real
    return conv[n - 1 : L][::-1]


def _sign_optimize(A0, time_limit, rng=None, q_values=(2, 4, 8), batch=8, label=""):
    """Discrete sign-gradient refinement of an anti-palindromic binary support."""
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(999)

    A0 = np.asarray(A0, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    m = int(A0.size)
    n = 2 * m
    L = 2 * n - 1

    A = A0.copy()
    S = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    S[A] = 1.0
    C = _fft_square(S).astype(np.int32)
    np.maximum(C, 0, out=C)

    Cmax = int(C.max())
    best_A = A.copy()
    best_C = C.copy()
    best_S = S.copy()
    best_max = Cmax
    best_count = int(np.count_nonzero(C == Cmax))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    q_idx = 0
    no_imp = 0
    max_no = 180
    resets = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        Cmax = int(C.max())
        if Cmax <= best_max:
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(C == Cmax))
            if Cmax < best_max or (Cmax == best_max and cnt < best_count):
                best_max = Cmax
                best_count = cnt
                best_A = A.copy()
                best_C = C.copy()
                best_S = S.copy()
                no_imp = 0
            else:
                no_imp += 1
        else:
            no_imp += 1

        if no_imp > max_no:
            resets += 1
            if resets > 40:
                break
            A = best_A.copy()
            C = best_C.copy()
            S = best_S.copy()
            for _ in range(int(rng.integers(3, 25))):
                r = int(rng.integers(m))
                _apply_sign_flip(A, C, S, n, r)
            no_imp = 0
            max_no = int(max_no * 1.3)
            continue

        q = int(q_values[min(q_idx, len(q_values) - 1)])
        Cmax_f = max(1.0, float(Cmax))
        Cf = C.astype(np.float64)
        W = (Cf / Cmax_f) ** float(q)
        W += 1e-12

        try:
            B = _corr_weights(S, W, n)
        except Exception:
            break

        p = A
        qpos = n - 1 - p
        delta = (
            2.0 * (B[qpos] - B[p])
            + W[2 * p]
            - 2.0 * W[n - 1]
            + W[2 * qpos]
        )

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)):
            A = best_A.copy()
            C = best_C.copy()
            S = best_S.copy()
            continue

        if np.any(delta < -1e-10):
            idxs = np.argsort(delta)[:batch]
            for idx in idxs:
                if delta[int(idx)] < -1e-12:
                    _apply_sign_flip(A, C, S, n, int(idx))
        else:
            for _ in range(int(rng.integers(1, 5))):
                r = int(rng.integers(m))
                _apply_sign_flip(A, C, S, n, r)

        # Remove any accidental negative entries caused by rare integer rounding.
        if C.min() < 0:
            np.maximum(C, 0, out=C)

        if no_imp > 100:
            q_idx = min(q_idx + 1, len(q_values) - 1)

        it += 1

    best_score = float(4.0 * int(best_max) / float(m))
    return best_A, best_score


def _pair_moment_opt(x0, time_limit, p=4, lr=0.12, rng=None, label=""):
    """
    Adam-style continuous optimizer for the anti-palindromic ansatz.
    Minimizes focused moments of u=(2n-1)*conv(a,a).
    """
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(1)

    m = int(np.asarray(x0).size)
    n = 2 * m
    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    x = np.clip(x, 1e-7, 1.0 - 1e-7)

    a = _pair_from_x(x)
    b = _fft_square(a)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * float(b.max()))
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_x = x.copy()

    moment = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    vel = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    beta1 = 0.9
    beta2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-8

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    it = 0
    no_imp = 0
    max_no = 450
    resets = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        b = _fft_square(a)
        score = float(2.0 * n * float(b.max()))

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_x = x.copy()
            no_imp = 0
        else:
            no_imp += 1

        if no_imp > max_no:
            resets += 1
            if resets > 30:
                break
            if best_score < float("inf"):
                x = best_x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.15, size=m)
            else:
                x = _init_x_random(m, rng)
            x = np.clip(x, 1e-7, 1.0 - 1e-7)
            a = _pair_from_x(x)
            moment = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            vel = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
            no_imp = 0
            max_no = int(max_no * 1.3)
            continue

        L = 2 * n - 1
        u = float(L) * b
        umax = float(u.max())
        if umax <= 1e-300:
            break

        uw = np.minimum(u, 100.0)
        w = (uw / (umax + 1e-300)) ** max(0.0, float(p) - 1.0)
        w += 1e-12

        grad_a = _fft_grad(w, a)
        gx = grad_a[:m] - grad_a[m:][::-1]
        gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(gx)))

        if not np.isfinite(gscale) or gscale < 1e-15:
            x = best_x + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=m)
            x = np.clip(x, 1e-7, 1.0 - 1e-7)
            a = _pair_from_x(x)
            continue

        g = gx / gscale
        it += 1
        b1t = beta1 ** it
        b2t = beta2 ** it
        moment = beta1 * moment + (1.0 - beta1) * g
        vel = beta2 * vel + (1.0 - beta2) * (g * g)
        mh = moment / (1.0 - b1t)
        vh = vel / (1.0 - b2t)

        x = x - float(lr) * mh / np.sqrt(vh + eps)
        x = np.clip(x, 1e-7, 1.0 - 1e-7)
        a = _pair_from_x(x)

    return best_a, best_score, best_x


def _full_moment_opt(a0, time_limit, p=4, lr=0.12, rng=None, label=""):
    """
    General non-negative moment optimizer in log/exponential-update form.
    Used for final cleanup outside the anti-palindromic ansatz.
    """
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(7)

    a = _clean_array(a0)
    n = int(a.size)

    b = _fft_square(a)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * float(b.max()))
    best_a = a.copy()

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    no_imp = 0
    max_no = 450
    resets = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < float(time_limit):
        b = _fft_square(a)
        score = float(2.0 * n * float(b.max()))

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_imp = 0
        else:
            no_imp += 1

        if no_imp > max_no:
            resets += 1
            if resets > 30:
                break
            if best_score < float("inf"):
                a = best_a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, size=n))
            else:
                a = _random_positive(n, rng)
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
            ss = float(a.sum())
            if ss <= 0.01:
                a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
            else:
                a /= ss
            no_imp = 0
            max_no = int(max_no * 1.3)
            continue

        L = 2 * n - 1
        u = float(L) * b
        umax = float(u.max())
        if umax <= 1e-300:
            break

        uw = np.minimum(u, 100.0)
        w = (uw / (umax + 1e-300)) ** max(0.0, float(p) - 1.0)
        w += 1e-12

        grad_a = _fft_grad(w, a)
        gd = grad_a - float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
        gscale = float(np.max(np.abs(gd)))

        if not np.isfinite(gscale) or gscale < 1e-15:
            a = best_a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=n))
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
            a /= float(a.sum())
            continue

        gd = gd / gscale
        with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
            a = a * np.exp(-float(lr) * gd)

        a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
        ss = float(a.sum())
        if ss <= 0.01:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        else:
            a /= ss

        # Occasionally inject fresh diversity to avoid extreme collapse.
        if (it % 300 == 0) and it > 0:
            a = 0.85 * a + 0.15 * _random_positive(n, rng)
            a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
            a /= float(a.sum())

        it += 1

    return best_a, best_score


def _safe_evaluate(seq):
    ev = globals().get("evaluate_sequence")
    if ev is None or not callable(ev):
        return float("inf")
    try:
        return float(ev(seq))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2350, **kwargs):
    seed = int(seed)
    total = max(60.0, min(float(budget_s), 2350.0))
    start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = start + total - 30.0

    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    best_x = None
    best_sign_A = None

    def _time_left():
        return hard_deadline - time.monotonic()

    print("starting improved anti-palindromic convolution optimizer")

    # Use any supplied starting sequence, if useful.
    given = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    given_a = None
    try:
        if isinstance(given, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)):
            g = _clean_array(given)
            if g.size > 1 and g.sum() > 0.01:
                sc = _score_norm(g)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = g.copy()
                if g.size <= 12000:
                    given_a = g.copy()
                print(f"given sequence internal score={sc:.6f} n={g.size}")
    except Exception:
        given_a = None

    rng_main = np.random.default_rng((seed * 1000003 + 17) % (2**32))
    rng_pair = np.random.default_rng((seed * 7919 + 999) % (2**32))

    # Primary anti-palindromic length. 8192 is a good tradeoff.
    m = 4096
    n = 2 * m

    # ---------- Greedy binary starts ----------
    task_deadline = hard_deadline - 300.0

    sign_cands = []
    for mode in range(4):
        if time.monotonic() >= task_deadline:
            break
        try:
            A, C, Cmax = _sign_greedy(m, mode, rng_pair)
            sc = 4.0 * float(Cmax) / float(m)
            sign_cands.append((sc, A, C, Cmax))
        except Exception as exc:
            print("greedy sign failed", repr(exc))

    sign_cands.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

    if len(sign_cands) > 0:
        best_sign_A = sign_cands[0][1].copy()

    # ---------- Discrete sign gradient refinement ----------
    for idx, (sc0, A0, C0, Cmax0) in enumerate(sign_cands[:2]):
        if time.monotonic() >= task_deadline - 20.0:
            break
        alloc = min(110.0, task_deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0)
        if alloc < 20.0:
            break
        print(
            f"sign gradient task idx={idx} alloc={alloc:.1f}s "
            f"initial_sign_score={sc0:.6f}"
        )
        try:
            Aus, sc = _sign_optimize(A0, alloc, rng_pair, label=f"sign{idx}")
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                # Exact binary conversion with tiny epsilon, then understand via score.
                x_bin = _sign_to_x(Aus, left_val=1.0 - 1e-7)
                a_bin = _pair_from_x(x_bin)
                sc_bin = _score_norm(a_bin)
                if np.isfinite(sc_bin) and sc_bin < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_bin
                    best_a = a_bin.copy()
                    best_x = x_bin.copy()
                    best_sign_A = Aus.copy()
                # Keep a softer binary for continuous polishing.
                x_soft = _sign_to_x(Aus, left_val=0.85)
                a_soft = _pair_from_x(x_soft)
                sc_soft = _score_norm(a_soft)
                if np.isfinite(sc_soft) and sc_soft < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_soft
                    best_a = a_soft.copy()
                    best_x = x_soft.copy()
        except Exception as exc:
            print("sign optimize failed", repr(exc))

    # ---------- Continuous anti-palindromic tasks ----------
    x_tasks = []

    if best_sign_A is not None:
        for lv in (0.92, 0.82, 0.68, 0.55):
            x_tasks.append((_sign_to_x(best_sign_A, left_val=lv), 170.0, 4.0, 0.12, f"sign{lv}"))
        x_tasks.append((_sign_to_x(best_sign_A, left_val=0.85), 140.0, 8.0, 0.07, "signH8"))

    for mode in range(4):
        x_tasks.append((_init_x_fourier(m, rng_pair, mode=mode), 120.0, 4.0, 0.13, f"fourier{mode}"))

    for k in (3, 5, 8, 16):
        x_tasks.append((_init_x_block(m, rng_pair, k=k, val=0.85), 85.0, 4.0, 0.17, f"block{k}"))

    x_tasks.append((_init_x_random(m, rng_pair), 80.0, 2.0, 0.12, "random"))
    x_tasks.append((_init_x_fourier(m, rng_pair, mode=1), 90.0, 8.0, 0.08, "fourierH1"))
    x_tasks.append((_init_x_fourier(m, rng_pair, mode=3), 90.0, 8.0, 0.07, "fourierH3"))

    # If the given sequence is anti-like, add it as a start.
    if given_a is not None and given_a.size == n:
        xg = np.clip(given_a[:m] * float(m), 1e-6, 1.0 - 1e-6)
        x_tasks.append((xg, 90.0, 6.0, 0.10, "given_pair"))

    for idx, (x0, alloc0, p, lr, tag) in enumerate(x_tasks):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= task_deadline:
            break
        alloc = min(float(alloc0), task_deadline - now)
        if alloc < 18.0:
            break

        print(f"pair task {tag} alloc={alloc:.1f}s p={p:g} lr={lr:g}")
        try:
            a_cand, sc_cand, x_cand = _pair_moment_opt(
                x0, alloc, p=p, lr=lr, rng=rng_pair, label=tag
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc_cand) and sc_cand < best_score:
                best_score = sc_cand
                best_a = a_cand.copy()
                best_x = x_cand.copy()
            print(f"pair task {tag} score={sc_cand:.6f} best={best_score:.6f}")
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"pair task {tag} failed", repr(exc))

    # ---------- Extra polish from current best continuous x ----------
    if best_x is not None and best_x.size == m:
        rem = task_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 70.0:
            alloc = min(150.0, rem)
            print(f"final pair polish alloc={alloc:.1f}s")
            try:
                a_cand, sc_cand, x_cand = _pair_moment_opt(
                    best_x, alloc, p=8.0, lr=0.06, rng=rng_pair, label="pairpolish"
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc_cand) and sc_cand < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_cand
                    best_a = a_cand.copy()
                    best_x = x_cand.copy()
                print(f"final pair polish score={sc_cand:.6f} best={best_score:.6f}")
            except Exception as exc:
                print("pair polish failed", repr(exc))

    # ---------- General full-vector polish ----------
    final_buffer = 60.0
    if best_a is not None:
        rem = hard_deadline - final_buffer - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 35.0:
            alloc = min(220.0, rem)
            print(f"full optimizer alloc={alloc:.1f}s n={best_a.size}")
            try:
                a_cand, sc_cand = _full_moment_opt(
                    best_a, alloc, p=6.0, lr=0.11, rng=rng_main, label="full"
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc_cand) and sc_cand < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_cand
                    best_a = a_cand.copy()
                print(f"full optimizer score={sc_cand:.6f} best={best_score:.6f}")
            except Exception as exc:
                print("full optimizer failed", repr(exc))

    # ---------- Fallback ----------
    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_score = 2.0

    best_a = _clean_array(best_a)
    final_seq = [float(v) for v in best_a]

    # Optional exact evaluation for confidence.
    if best_a.size <= 12000 and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 5.0:
        exact = _safe_evaluate(final_seq)
        if np.isfinite(exact):
            print(f"final internal={best_score:.6f} exact={exact:.6f} n={best_a.size}")
        else:
            print(f"final internal={best_score:.6f} exact failed n={best_a.size}")
    else:
        print(f"final internal={best_score:.6f} n={best_a.size}")

    return final_seq
```
